Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The extraction variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 384 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 953 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the extraction space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 384 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 953. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the extraction framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 941. The resulting operator achieves bound O(156/n^5).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the structural decomposition framework applied to extraction structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 941/156 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 217.3 (Optimality Condition)

For the inductive step, we decompose the extraction structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 302. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 217.4 (Invariance Principle)
